value === 'string' && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(MODEL_SPEC_REGISTRY, value);\n+}\n+\n+export function coerceSelectedModel(value: unknown): SelectedModel {\n+  return isSelectedModel(value) resolveSelectedModelFromModelName(modelName?: string | null): SelectedModel {\n+  if (!modelName) {\n+    return 'wan-2.2';\n+  }\n+\n+  const matchedEntry = (Object.entries(MODEL_SPEC_REGISTRY) as Array<[SelectedModel, ModelSpec]>)\n+    .find(([, spec]) => spec.defaultWorkerModelName === modelName);\n+\n+  return matchedEntry?.[0] ?? 'wan-2.2';\n+}\n+\n+export function getInferenceStepRange(model: SelectedModel): { min: number; max: number } {\n+  const [min, max] = getModelSpec(model).stepRange;\n+  return { min, max };\n+}\n+\n+function resolveExecutionMode(\n+  spec: ModelSpec,\n+  intent: ResolveGenerationPolicyIntent,\n+): ExecutionMode {\n+  if (spec.modelFamily === 'ltx') {\n+    return 'i2v';\n+  }\n+\n+  if (intent.guidanceKind === 'uni3c') {\n+    return 'i2v';\n+  }\n+\n+  if (\n+    intent.guidanceKind === 'flow'\n+    || intent.guidanceKind === 'canny'\n+    || intent.guidanceKind === 'depth'\n+    || intent.guidanceKind === 'raw'\n+  ) {\n+    return 'vace';\n+  }\n+\n+  return intent.requestedExecutionMode;\n+}\n+\n+export function resolveContinuationPolicy(\n+  spec: ModelSpec,\n+  intent: ResolveGenerationPolicyIntent,\n+): ResolvedContinuationPolicy {\n+  const executionMode = resolveExecutionMode(spec, intent);\n+  const strategy = intent.smoothContinuations\n+    ? spec.continuationByExecutionMode[executionMode]\n+    : undefined;\n+\n+  if (!strategy) {\n+    return {\n+      enabled: false,\n+      overlapFrames: DEFAULT_FRAME_OVERLAP,\n+      maxOutputFrames: spec.maxFrames,\n+    };\n+  }\n+\n+  return {\n+    enabled: true,\n+    strategy,\n+    overlapFrames: CONTINUATION_LIMITS[strategy].overlapFrames,\n+    maxOutputFrames: CONTINUATION_LIMITS[strategy].maxOutputFrames,\n+  };\n+}\n+\n+export function resolveGenerationPolicy(\n+  spec: ModelSpec,\n+  intent: ResolveGenerationPolicyIntent,\n+): ResolvedGenerationPolicy {\n+  const travelMode = resolveExecutionMode(spec, intent);\n+  const continuation = resolveContinuationPolicy(spec, intent);\n+\n+  return {\n+    travelMode,\n+    continuation,\n+    frameOverlap: continuation.overlapFrames,\n+  };\n+}\n*** End Patch\n"

{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Reigh-Worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py\n@@\n def _resolve_generation_inputs(ctx: SegmentContext, task_id: str, main_output_dir_base: Path) -> GenerationInputs:\n@@\n     )\n \n \n+def _resolve_parent_generation_id(ctx: SegmentContext) -> str | None:\n+    return _get_param(\n+        \"parent_generation_id\",\n+        ctx.individual_params,\n+        ctx.segment_params,\n+        ctx.orchestrator_details,\n+        default=None,\n+        prefer_truthy=True,\n+    )\n+\n+\n+def _download_predecessor_video(\n+    predecessor_output_url: str,\n+    segment_processing_dir: Path,\n+    *,\n+    prefix: str,\n+) -> str | None:\n+    if not predecessor_output_url.startswith(\"http\"):\n+        return predecessor_output_url\n+\n+    try:\n+        local_filename = Path(predecessor_output_url).name\n+        local_download_path = segment_processing_dir / f\"{prefix}_{local_filename}\"\n+        if not local_download_path.exists():\n+            download_file(predecessor_output_url, segment_processing_dir, local_download_path.name)\n+        return str(local_download_path)\n+    except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError):\n+        return None\n+\n+\n+def _extract_prefix_video(\n+    predecessor_video_path: str,\n+    segment_processing_dir: Path,\n+    *,\n+    segment_idx: int,\n+    frames_needed: int,\n+    prefix: str,\n+) -> str:\n+    from source.media.video import (\n+        extract_frame_range_to_video as extract_frame_range_to_video,\n+        get_video_frame_count_and_fps as get_video_frame_count_and_fps,\n+    )\n+\n+    pred_frames, pred_fps = get_video_frame_count_and_fps(predecessor_video_path)\n+    if not pred_frames or pred_frames <= 0:\n+        return predecessor_video_path\n+\n+    start_frame = max(0, int(pred_frames) - int(frames_needed))\n+    trimmed_prefix_path = segment_processing_dir / (\n+        f\"{prefix}_{segment_idx:02d}_last{frames_needed}frames_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}.mp4\"\n+    )\n+    trimmed_result = extract_frame_range_to_video(\n+        input_video_path=predecessor_video_path,\n+        output_video_path=str(trimmed_prefix_path),\n+        start_frame=start_frame,\n+        end_frame=None,\n+        fps=float(pred_fps) if pred_fps and pred_fps > 0 else 16.0,\n+    )\n+    return str(trimmed_result)\n+\n+\n def _resolve_image_references(ctx: SegmentContext, gen: GenerationInputs, task_id: str, is_standalone: bool) -> ImageRefs:\n*** End Patch\n"
}

> AGENT

Implemented the plan across both repos.

On the frontend, I added the model capability registry in [modelCapabilities.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/modelCapabilities.ts), pushed `continuation_config` through the shared contracts and payload builders in [requestBody.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/services/generateVideo/requestBody.ts), [segmentTaskPayload.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/tasks/segmentTaskPayload.ts), and [submitSegmentTask.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/domains/media-lightbox/components/submitSegmentTask.ts), and finished the per-segment smooth-continuation UI/state handling in [SegmentSettingsForm.tsx](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/components/SegmentSettingsForm/SegmentSettingsForm.tsx).

On the worker, I added the normalized policy parser in [generation_policy.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Reigh-Worker/source/core/params/generation_policy.py), wired continuation-aware routing and LTX prefix `video_source` handling into [task_registry.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Reigh-Worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py), switched orchestration/dependency/stitch logic over to policy-based gating in [orchestrator.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Reigh-Worker/source/task_handlers/travel/orchestrator.py), and added standalone predecessor fallback lookup in [task_dependencies.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Reigh-Worker/source/core/db/task_dependencies.py) and [guide_builder.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Reigh-Worker/source/task_handlers/travel/guide_builder.py). I also preserved existing VACE mask behavior while making LTX prefix continuation skip overlap-mask logic.

Verification passed:
- `npx tsc --noEmit`
- `python -m py_compile ...` on all modified worker files
- `npx vitest run --config config/testing/vitest.config.ts ...` with 25 tests passing across the touched frontend surfaces

I did not run a live end-to-end generation through the actual model stack, so the remaining risk is runtime-only behavior for LTX continuation quality rather than compile/test correctness. The worker repo also showed unrelated modifications in [generation.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Reigh-Worker/source/task_handlers/join/generation.py) and [final_stitch.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Reigh-Worker/source/task_handlers/join/final_stitch.py); I left those untouched.

> AGENT

Fixed the crash.

`useShotEditorLayoutModel` was reading `settings.phaseConfigSettings.generationTypeMode`, but `phaseConfigSettings` was never passed into the `settings` object from [useShotEditorController.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/useShotEditorController.ts). I added that slice to the layout-model params in [useShotEditorLayoutModel.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/controllers/useShotEditorLayoutModel.ts) and passed it through at the callsite.

Verified with `npx tsc --noEmit`. Reload the tool and that `generationTypeMode` null-access should be gone.

> AGENT

**Findings**
- High: the worker’s predecessor resolver is accidentally neutered. [`task_dependencies.py#L368`](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Reigh-Worker/source/core/db/task_dependencies.py#L368) now returns from `get_predecessor_output_via_edge_function()` immediately after the “Supabase config missing” branch, while the original edge-call body only survives as dead code starting at [`task_dependencies.py#L471`](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Reigh-Worker/source/core/db/task_dependencies.py#L471). In a normal configured worker, that function now always returns `(None, None)`, so dependency-based predecessor lookup is broken for both the new continuation path and any existing callers.
- Medium: the standalone fallback predecessor lookup is not branch-safe, so it can continue from the wrong segment variant. [`task_dependencies.py#L441`](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Reigh-Worker/source/core/db/task_dependencies.py#L441) only matches on `parent_generation_id` plus `child_order`/`segment_index`, but the task payload still carries branch-specific IDs like [`segmentTaskPayload.ts#L189`](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/tasks/segmentTaskPayload.ts#L189) `child_generation_id` and [`segmentTaskPayload.ts#L225`](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/shared/lib/tasks/segmentTaskPayload.ts#L225) `pair_shot_generation_id`. If there are multiple regenerated variants under the same parent generation, this fallback will pick the newest completed predecessor, not necessarily the correct sibling branch.
- Medium: the smooth-continuation frame cap is still enforced with legacy state logic, so LTX can submit oversized segments. [`useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers.ts#L213`](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/settings/useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers.ts#L213) still hard-clamps to `77` on enable, [`VideoTravelSettingsProvider.tsx#L138`](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/providers/VideoTravelSettingsProvider.tsx#L138) restores per-model frame substate without reapplying the continuation cap, and [`generateVideoService.ts#L232`](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/services/generateVideoService.ts#L232) uses `batchVideoFrames` verbatim when building pair configs. That means LTX + SC can still send 77/97-frame segments instead of the intended 73-frame max.

**Assumptions**
- I reviewed the continuation/model-capability implementation only, not unrelated dirty files in the repo.
- I treated compile/test-clean code as necessary but not sufficient; the findings above are logic/runtime issues.

**Checks Run**
- `npx tsc --noEmit`
- `python -m py_compile` on the modified worker files
- Targeted Vitest slice with the repo config: 25 tests passed

If you want, I can fix these three issues next and re-run the same verification pass.

> AGENT

Fixed the batch crash and the three continuation issues.

On the frontend, [BatchSettingsForm.tsx](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/BatchSettingsForm.tsx) now receives/destructures `smoothContinuations` correctly, and I added a shared continuation-aware frame clamp in [modelCapabilities.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/modelCapabilities.ts). That clamp is now enforced in [useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/settings/useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers.ts), [VideoTravelSettingsProvider.tsx](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/providers/VideoTravelSettingsProvider.tsx), and [generateVideoService.ts](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/services/generateVideoService.ts), so smooth-continuation limits are applied on toggle, mode switch, model switch, persisted substate restore, and request submission. I also passed the missing model/continuation props through [VideoGenerationModalSections.tsx](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/VideoGenerationModalSections.tsx) and [SharedGenerationView.tsx](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/SharedGenerationView.tsx).

On the worker, [task_dependencies.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Reigh-Worker/source/core/db/task_dependencies.py) now actually runs the predecessor edge lookup again instead of returning early, and the standalone fallback is branch-aware by filtering on `child_generation_id`. I threaded that through [task_dependencies_queries.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Reigh-Worker/source/core/db/dependencies/task_dependencies_queries.py), [task_registry.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Reigh-Worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py), and [guide_builder.py](/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/Reigh-Worker/source/task_handlers/travel/guide_builder.py).

Verified with `npx tsc --noEmit`, `npx vitest run --config config/testing/vitest.config.ts ...` (123 passing tests across the touched frontend suites), and `python -m py_compile` on the modified worker files. I did not run a live end-to-end generation, so the remaining risk is runtime-only behavior in the actual model pipeline rather than type/test correctness.

> DEVELOPER

is this beautifully engineered now?

> AGENT

No.

It’s materially safer now: the runtime crashes are fixed, continuation policy is enforced consistently across UI state and submission, and the worker predecessor lookup is no longer broken or branch-ambiguous.

But “beautifully engineered” would require more consolidation than this code currently has. The main remaining gap is that continuation/model policy is still spread across several frontend layers and mirrored again in the worker. It’s coherent enough now, but not yet minimal or elegant. The next bar-up step would be to centralize frame-policy normalization behind one frontend state adapter and one worker payload reader so the rules stop leaking across handlers, providers, forms, and task routing.
